# Downer EDI Limited
## Annual Report 2023

**Ticker:** DOW | **Exchange:** ASX | **Currency:** AUD
**Sector:** Engineering & Infrastructure Services | **Country:** Australia

---

## Company Overview

Downer EDI Limited is a leading engineering & infrastructure services company headquartered in Australia. The company serves customers across multiple markets and geographies, delivering high-quality products and services. As of fiscal year 2023, Downer EDI Limited employs approximately 33,000 people worldwide and operates across multiple business segments.

The company's mission is to create sustainable value for shareholders, customers, and communities through operational excellence, innovation, and responsible business practices.

---

## Letter to Shareholders

Dear Shareholders,

Fiscal year 2023 was a defining year for Downer EDI Limited. Despite a challenging macroeconomic environment characterized by inflationary pressures, geopolitical uncertainty, and evolving customer demands, we delivered solid financial results and made meaningful progress on our strategic priorities.

Revenue for the year was AUD 12,841 million, compared to AUD 11,439.4 million in the prior year. Net income was AUD 124 million (+7.9% year-over-year). These results reflect the resilience of our business model and the dedication of our global workforce.

Looking ahead, we remain confident in our ability to execute on our strategic plan and deliver long-term value.

Sincerely,
The Board of Directors

---

## Business Segments

### Segment 1 — Core Operations

The core operations segment represents the primary revenue-generating activities of Downer EDI Limited. In fiscal 2023, this segment contributed approximately 7,961.4 AUD million in revenue, representing 62.0% of total company revenue. Operating margins in this segment improved by 80 basis points year-over-year, driven by operational efficiencies and pricing actions.

Key performance indicators for the core operations segment:
- Revenue growth: 4.5% year-over-year
- Operating margin: 27.7%
- Customer retention rate: 90.3%
- New customer acquisitions: 37,693

### Segment 2 — Growth Initiatives

The growth initiatives segment encompasses newer business lines and expansion activities. Revenue from this segment totalled 3,595.5 AUD million in fiscal 2023, up 10.0% from the prior year. The company continues to invest selectively in high-return growth opportunities within this segment.

### Segment 3 — Strategic & Other

This segment includes corporate activities, shared services, and strategic investments. The segment recorded 1,284.1 AUD million in revenue and contributed positively to overall company profitability through cost discipline and shared service optimization.

---

## Management Discussion & Analysis

### Revenue Analysis

Total revenue for fiscal year 2023 was AUD 12,841 million, compared to AUD 11,439.4 million in fiscal 2022, representing a change of 12.3%. The revenue performance reflects:

- Strong demand across core product and service lines
- Geographic expansion into new markets
- Pricing actions implemented in response to cost inflation
- Foreign currency headwinds of approximately 285.5 AUD million

### Profitability Analysis

Gross profit for fiscal 2023 was AUD 2362.7 million, resulting in a gross margin of 18.4%. The gross margin reflects efficient cost management and improved pricing realization.

Operating expenses totalled AUD 5,101.9 million, including:
- Research and development: AUD 642.6 million
- Sales and marketing: AUD 1,739.2 million
- General and administrative: AUD 873.3 million

Net income for the period was AUD 124 million, compared to AUD 114.9 million in the prior year.

### Cash Flow Analysis

Cash flow from operations for fiscal 2023 was AUD 412 million, compared to AUD 375 million in the prior year. The improvement reflects higher earnings and effective working capital management.

Capital expenditures for the year amounted to AUD 198 million, primarily invested in:
- Property, plant and equipment upgrades: AUD 95.0 million
- Technology infrastructure: AUD 63.4 million
- Capacity expansion: AUD 39.6 million

Free cash flow (operating cash flow less capital expenditures) was AUD 214 million.

### Balance Sheet Analysis

Total assets at year-end were AUD 6,234 million, compared to AUD 5,860 million at the end of the prior fiscal year. The increase reflects organic growth and strategic investments.

Total liabilities were AUD 3,651 million. Total equity was AUD 2,951 million.

The company maintained a strong liquidity position with AUD 396 million in cash and cash equivalents at year-end.

---

## Risk Factors

The company faces various risks and uncertainties that could materially affect its business, financial condition, and results of operations:

### Macroeconomic Risks
- Inflationary pressures on input costs and operating expenses
- Interest rate fluctuations affecting financing costs and customer demand
- Foreign currency exchange rate volatility impacting reported results
- Recessionary conditions in key markets

### Operational Risks
- Supply chain disruptions and raw material availability
- Technology system failures and cybersecurity threats
- Talent attraction and retention in competitive labour markets
- Regulatory compliance across multiple jurisdictions

### Strategic Risks
- Competitive landscape changes and market share erosion
- Failure to execute on strategic initiatives and growth plans
- Integration risks associated with acquisitions
- Disruptive technologies altering industry dynamics

### Financial Risks
- Credit risk from customer and counterparty exposures
- Liquidity risk and access to capital markets
- Impairment of goodwill and intangible assets
- Tax law changes in jurisdictions of operation

---

## Corporate Governance

### Board of Directors

The Board of Directors of Downer EDI Limited consists of 12 members, of whom 7 are independent non-executive directors. The Board is committed to maintaining the highest standards of corporate governance and ethical conduct.

Board committees include:
- Audit and Risk Committee
- Remuneration and Nomination Committee
- Sustainability and ESG Committee
- Strategy and Investment Committee

## ESG & Sustainability

Downer EDI Limited is committed to sustainable business practices and responsible environmental stewardship. In fiscal 2023, the company made significant progress across its ESG priorities:

### Environmental
- Reduced Scope 1 and Scope 2 greenhouse gas emissions by 16.5% year-over-year
- Increased renewable energy consumption to 41.6% of total energy use
- Achieved zero-waste-to-landfill status at 7 manufacturing facilities
- Reduced water consumption by 8.0% through efficiency initiatives

### Social
- Employee engagement score: 74% (up from 71% in prior year)
- Total training hours delivered: 2,009,089 hours
- Women in senior leadership roles: 34.3%
- Lost time injury frequency rate: 2.77

### Governance
- Board independence: 70%
- Ethics and compliance training completion rate: 99%
- Supplier code of conduct signatories: 90% of supply base

---

## Mergers, Acquisitions & Strategic Transactions

During fiscal 2023, Downer EDI Limited evaluated various strategic opportunities. The company maintains a disciplined approach to capital allocation and will pursue acquisitions only where they meet strict financial and strategic criteria.

---

## Capital Returns to Shareholders

### Share Buyback Program

During fiscal 2023, the Board of Downer EDI Limited approved a share buyback program of up to AUD 39 million. Under this program, the company repurchased 7.6 million shares at an average price of AUD 16.42 per share.

The buyback program reflects the Board's confidence in the company's financial position and long-term prospects, and its commitment to returning excess capital to shareholders.

---

## Workforce Changes

As part of its ongoing operational efficiency program, Downer EDI Limited reduced its total headcount by approximately 800 employees during fiscal 2023. This reduction was achieved through a combination of voluntary separations, natural attrition, and targeted redundancies. The company provided severance packages and career transition support to affected employees in line with applicable employment laws and the company's values.

## Notes to the Financial Statements

### Note 1 — Significant Accounting Policies

The financial statements have been prepared in accordance with International Financial Reporting Standards (IFRS) as adopted by the relevant jurisdiction. The financial statements are presented in AUD millions unless otherwise stated.

**Revenue Recognition:** Revenue is recognised when control of goods or services is transferred to the customer at the amount that reflects the consideration to which the entity expects to be entitled.

**Property, Plant and Equipment:** PPE is stated at cost less accumulated depreciation and impairment losses. Depreciation is calculated on a straight-line basis over the estimated useful lives of the assets.

**Goodwill:** Goodwill is tested for impairment annually or more frequently if events or changes in circumstances indicate a potential impairment. Goodwill is stated at cost less accumulated impairment losses.

**Financial Instruments:** Financial instruments are recognised initially at fair value. Subsequent measurement depends on the classification of the financial instrument.

### Note 2 — Segment Information

The company operates through three reportable segments as described in the Management Discussion and Analysis section. Segment results are reported on a consistent basis with internal management reporting.

### Note 3 — Related Party Transactions

During fiscal 2023, the company entered into various transactions with related parties in the ordinary course of business. All related party transactions were conducted on arm's length terms.

### Note 4 — Subsequent Events

No material events have occurred subsequent to the balance sheet date that would require adjustment to or disclosure in these financial statements.

**Opinion:** In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements present fairly, in all material respects, the financial position of Downer EDI Limited and its subsidiaries as at December 31, 2023, and their financial performance and cash flows for the year then ended, in accordance with International Financial Reporting Standards.
